public S3KpiWriter(IAmazonS3 s3, string bucket, ILogger<S3KpiWriter> logger)\n   395→        {\n   396→            _s3     = s3     ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(s3));\n   397→ public async ValueTask WriteAsync(\n   402→            IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult> rows,\n   403→            CancellationToken                 ct = default)\n   404→        {\n   405→       Key         = objectKey,\n   418→                ContentBody = jsonLines,\n   419→                ContentType = \"application/json\"\n   420→            };\n   421→\n   422→            await RetryPolicy.ExecuteAsync(\n   423→                async () => await _s3.PutObjectAsync(request, ct).ConfigureAwait(false),\n   424→                ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   425→\n   426→            _logger.LogDebug(\"Wrote {RowCount} KPI rows to s3://{Bucket}/{Key}.\", rows.Count(), _bucket, objectKey);\n   427→        }\n   428→\n   429→        public ValueTask DisposeAsync() => internal sealed class AmazonS3RetryPolicy\n   436→    {\n   437→        private static readonly TimeSpan[] BackOffSchedule =\n   438→        {\n   439→            TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100),\n   440→            TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(250),\n   441→            TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(500),\n   442→            TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1),\n   443→            TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2)\n   444→        };\n   445→\n   446→        public async Task ExecuteAsync(Func<Task> operation, CancellationToken ct)\n   447→        {\n   448→            var attempt = 0;\n   449→            Exception? lastError = null;\n   450→\n   451→            foreach (var delay in BackOffSchedule.Append(TimeSpan.Zero))\n   452→            {\n   453→                try\n   454→                {\n   455→                    ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n   456→                    await operation().ConfigureAwait(false);\n   457→                    return; // success\n   458→                }\n   459→                catch (Exception ex) when (IsTransient(ex))\n   460→                {\n   461→                    lastError = ex;\n   462→                    attempt++;\n   463→                    if (delay > TimeSpan.Zero)\n   464→                        await Task.Delay(delay, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   465→                }\n   466→            }\n   467→\n   468→            throw new InvalidOperationException(\n   469→                $\"S3 operation failed after {attempt} retries.\", lastError);\n   470→        }\n   471→\n   472→        private static bool IsTransient(Exception ex)\n   473→        {\n   474→            // TODO: Add richer fault-analysis; for now assume all AmazonS3Exception with 5xx are transient.\n   475→            return ex is AmazonS3Exception s3Ex && (int)s3Ex.StatusCode >= 500;\n   476→        }\n   477→    }\n   478→\n   479→    #endregion\n   480→}\n   481→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would The module is designed to execute inside an AWS Lambda but is consciously decoupled\n//  from the hosting environment (no direct dependency on Amazon.Lambda.* packages) so it can also be\n//  reused by Glue, Fargate, or on-prem batch jobs.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Concurrent;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Diagnostics;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing System.Threading;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\n\nusing Amazon.S3;\nusing Amazon.S3.Model;\n\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;\n\nnamespace PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations\n{\n    #region Public DTOs / Contracts -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Thin wrapper around any raw event that enters the warehouse.\n    /// Envelopes allow us to standardise metadata required for transformation,\n    /// without rewriting every single ingestion lambda when an attribute is added later on.\n    /// </summary>\n    /// <remarks>\n    /// The schema mirrors the one emitted by the <c>EventIngressProcessor</c> lambda.\n    /// </remarks>\n    public sealed record EventEnvelope\n    {\n        public required string EventId          { get; -------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Coordinates incoming event stream and dispatches to registered KPI strategies.\n    /// Takes care of parallelism, back-pressure, exception isolation, and durability (S3 write).\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class KpiTransformationEngine : IAsyncDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly IReadOnlyList<IKpiCalculationStrategy> _strategies;\n        private readonly IS3KpiWriter                           _writer;\n        private readonly ILogger<KpiTransformationEngine>       _logger;\n        private readonly SemaphoreSlim                          _parallelism;\n\n        public KpiTransformationEngine(\n            IEnumerable<IKpiCalculationStrategy> strategies,\n            IS3KpiWriter                           writer,\n            ILogger<KpiTransformationEngine>       logger,\n            int                                    maxDegreeOfParallelism = 8)\n        {\n            _strategies   = strategies?.ToList() ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(strategies));\n            _writer       = writer     ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(writer));\n            _logger       = logger     ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(logger));\n            if (!_strategies.Any())\n                throw new ArgumentException(\"At least one KPI strategy must be provided.\", nameof(strategies));\n\n            _parallelism  = new SemaphoreSlim(maxDegreeOfParallelism, maxDegreeOfParallelism);\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Orchestrates transformation for a batch of events.\n        /// </summary>\n        public async ValueTask ExecuteAsync(\n            IEnumerable<EventEnvelope> events,\n            CancellationToken         ct = default)\n        {\n            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(events);\n\n            var tasks = new List<Task>();\n\n            foreach (var envelope in events)\n            {\n                await _parallelism.WaitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                // Fire-and-forget – safe because we await all tasks in the list below\n                tasks.Add(Task.Run(async () =>\n                {\n                    try\n                    {\n                        await DispatchToStrategiesAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    }\n                    catch (Exception ex)\n                    {\n                        _logger.LogError(ex,\n                            \"Uncaught                  // Bubble up to trigger Lambda DLQ – do NOT swallow\n                        throw;\n                    }\n                    finally\n                    {\n                        _parallelism.Release();\n                    }\n                }, ct));\n            }\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(tasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n\n        private async Task DispatchToStrategiesAsync(EventEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n\n            var interestedStrategies = _strategies.Where(s => s.CanHandle(envelope)).ToArray();\n            if (interestedStrategies.Length == 0)\n            {\n                _logger.LogDebug(\"No KPI strategy registered for event {EventType}.\", envelope.EventType);\n                return;\n            }\n\n            var kpiRows = new ConcurrentBag<KpiCalculationResult>();\n\n            // Parallel but limited to #strategies so trivial overhead\n            var strategyTasks = interestedStrategies.Select(async strategy =>\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    var rows = await strategy.ProcessAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    foreach (var row in rows) kpiRows.Add(row);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                        \"Strategy {KpiStrategy} failed while processing EventId={EventId}. Skipping strategy.\",\n                        strategy.KpiName, envelope.EventId);\n                }\n            });\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(strategyTasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            // Commit results to the Data Lake (S3, parquet files partitioned by YYYY/MM/DD)\n            if (kpiRows.Count > 0)\n            {\n                await _writer.WriteAsync(kpiRows, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            }\n\n            _logger.LogInformation(\n                \"Processed EventId={EventId} in {ElapsedMs:n0} ms with {RowCount} KPI rows.\",\n                envelope.EventId, sw.ElapsedMilliseconds, kpiRows.Count);\n        }\n\n        public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n        {\n            _parallelism?.Dispose();\n            if (_writer is IAsyncDisposable asyncDisposable)\n                await asyncDisposable.DisposeAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Strategy Implementations ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Calculates the ratio between focus time and total working hours on a per-user, per-day basis.\n    /// Source events:\n    ///   -                  KpiName        = KpiName,\n                    WindowStartUtc = date,\n                    WindowEndUtc   = date.AddDays(1),\n                    Value          = durationMinutes, // later aggregated to obtain total focus time\n                    Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n                    {\n                        [\"user_id\"] = userId,\n                        [\"metric\"]  = \"focus_minutes\"\n                    }\n                };\n\n                return new[] { result };\n            }\n\n            if (envelope.EventType == CalendarEventType &&\n                envelope.Metadata.TryGetValue(\"DurationMinutes\", out var calendarDurationStr) &&\n                int.TryParse(calendarDurationStr, out var meetingMinutes))\n            {\n                var userId = envelope.Metadata[\"OrganizerId\"]!;\n                var date   = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime.Date;\n\n                var result = new KpiCalculationResult\n                {\n                    KpiName        = KpiName,\n                    WindowStartUtc = date,\n                    WindowEndUtc   = date.AddDays(1),\n                    Value          = meetingMinutes,\n                    Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n                    {\n                        [\"user_id\"] = userId,\n                        [\"metric\"]  = \"meeting_minutes\"\n                    }\n                };\n\n                return new[] { result };\n            }\n\n            return Array.Empty<KpiCalculationResult>();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Computes deployment lead-time (commit → production) on a per-repo basis.\n    /// Maintains a small in-memory buffer which is flushed every <see cref=\"FlushInterval\"/>.\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy : IKpiCalculationStrategy, IDisposable\n    {\n        private const string CommitEventType     = \"CodeCommitPushed\";\n        private const string ProdDeployEventType = \"CdPipelineDeployed\";\n\n        private readonly TimeSpan                               FlushInterval     = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);\n        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, DateTime> _commitTimestamps = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);\n        private readonly Timer                                  _flushTimer;\n        private readonly ILogger                                _logger;\n\n        public string KpiName => \"deployment_lead_time\";\n\n        public FlushInterval);\n        }\n\n        public bool CanHandle(EventEnvelope envelope)\n            => envelope.EventType is CommitEventType or ProdDeployEventType;\n\n        public async      {\n                case CommitEventType:\n                    {\n                        var commitSha  = envelope.Metadata[\"CommitSha\"]!;\n                        _commitTimestamps[commitSha] envelope.Metadata[\"CommitSha\"]!;\n                        if (_commitTimestamps.TryRemove(commitSha, out var commitTime))\n                        {\n                            var leadTimeMinutes return new[] { kpiRow };\n                        }\n                        break;\n                    }\n            }\n\n            return Array.Empty<KpiCalculationResult>();\n        }\n\n        private void FlushExpired()\n        {\n            var threshold = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(-24); // assume deploy happens within 24 h\n            foreach (var kvp in _commitTimestamps)\n            {\n                if (kvp.Value < threshold && _commitTimestamps.TryRemove(kvp.Key, out _))\n                {\n                    _logger.LogDebug(\n                        \"Dropped stale commit _flushTimer.Dispose();\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Persistence Layer -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Simple _logger;\n\n        private static readonly AmazonS3RetryPolicy RetryPolicy = new();\n\n        public S3KpiWriter(IAmazonS3 s3, string bucket, ILogger<S3KpiWriter> logger)\n        {\n            _s3     = s3     ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(s3));\n            _bucket = bucket ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(bucket));\n            _logger = logger ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(logger));\n        }\n\n        public async ValueTask WriteAsync(\n            IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult> rows,\n            CancellationToken                 ct = default)\n        {\n            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(rows);\n\n            var nowUtc     = DateTime.UtcNow;\n            var partition  = $\"{nowUtc:yyyy/MM/dd}\";\n            var objectKey  = $\"kpi/partition_date={partition}/{Guid.NewGuid():N}.jsonl\";\n\n            // Serialise as ND-JSON\n            var jsonLines  = string.Join('\\n', rows.Select(row => System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(row)));\n\n            var request = new PutObjectRequest\n            {\n                BucketName  = _bucket,\n                Key         = objectKey,\n                ContentBody = jsonLines,\n                ContentType = \"application/json\"\n            };\n\n            await RetryPolicy.ExecuteAsync(\n                async () => await operations.\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class AmazonS3RetryPolicy\n    {\n        private static readonly TimeSpan[] BackOffSchedule =\n        {\n            TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100),\n            TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(250),\n            TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(500),\n            TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1),\n            TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2)\n        };\n\n        public async Task ExecuteAsync(Func<Task> operation, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            var attempt = 0;\n            Exception? lastError = null;\n\n            foreach (var delay in BackOffSchedule.Append(TimeSpan.Zero))\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n                    await operation().ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    return; // success\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex) when (IsTransient(ex))\n                {\n                    lastError = ex;\n                    attempt++;\n                    if (delay > TimeSpan.Zero)\n                        await Task.Delay(delay, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                }\n            }\n\n            throw new InvalidOperationException(\n                $\"S3 operation failed after {attempt} retries.\", lastError);\n        }\n\n        private static bool IsTransient(Exception ex)\n        {\n            // TODO: Add richer fault-analysis; for now assume all AmazonS3Exception with 5xx are transient.\n            return ex is AmazonS3Exception s3Ex && (int)s3Ex.StatusCode >= 500;\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n}\n```","numLines":481,"startLine":1,"totalLines":481}}}

ratio,\n                totalFocusHours,\n                totalWorkingHours\n            });\n\n            return new KpiComputationResult(\n                context.TenantId,\n                \"focus_time_ratio\",\n                context.From,\n                context.To,\n                payloadJson);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Calculates Deployment Lead Time:\n    /// Time elapsed between code commit and successful production deployment.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy : IKpiComputationStrategy\n    {\n        public string StrategyId => \"deployment-lead-time\";\n\n        public async Task<KpiComputationResult> ComputeAsync(\n            KpiComputationContext context,\n            ILogger logger,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            await Task.Yield();\n\n            var leadTimes = new List<TimeSpan>();\n\n            // Naive sample implementation\n            var commits     = context.RawEvents.OfType<CommitEvent>().OrderBy(c => c.Timestamp).ToList();\n            var deployments = context.RawEvents.OfType<DeploymentEvent>().OrderBy(d => d.Timestamp).ToList();\n\n            foreach (var commit in commits)\n            {\n                cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n\n                var deploy = deployments.FirstOrDefault(d => d.CommitId == commit.CommitId);\n                if (deploy != null)\n                {\n                    leadTimes.Add(deploy.Timestamp - commit.Timestamp);\n KpiComputationResult(\n                context.TenantId,\n                \"deployment_lead_time\",\n                context.From,\n                context.To,\n                payloadJson);\n        }\n\n        private static TimeSpan Percentile(IReadOnlyList<TimeSpan> data, double percentile)\n        {\n            if (data.Count == 0) return TimeSpan.Zero;\n\n            var ordered = data.OrderBy(x => x).ToArray();\n            var index   = (int)Math.Ceiling(percentile * ordered.Length) - 1;\n            index       = Math.Clamp(index, 0, ordered.Length - 1);\n\n            return ordered[index];\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Calculates Meeting Cost Index – total salary cost of meetings held in the timeframe.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class KpiComputationContext context,\n            ILogger logger,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            await Task.Yield();\n\n            var hourlyRate      = context.Parameters.TryGetValue(\"hourlyRateUSD\", out var rateElm) &&\n                                  rateElm.TryGetDecimal(out var rateUsd) ? rateUsd : 90m;\n\n            decimal totalCost = 0m;\n            int     meetings  = 0;\n\n            foreach (var meeting in context.RawEvents.OfType<MeetingEvent>())\n            {\n                cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n\n                var durationHours = meeting.Duration.TotalHours;\n                var attendeeCount = meeting.Attendees.Count;\n\n                totalCost += (decimal)durationHours * attendeeCount * hourlyRate;\n                meetings++;\n            }\n\n            var payloadJson = JsonSerializer.SerializeToElement(new\n            {\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Strategy that emits raw focus-time minutes for Focus activities.\n    /// Additional ratio aggregation is done downstream in Athena/QuickSight.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class FocusTimeStrategy : IMetricComputationStrategy\n    {\n        private static readonly MetricDataPoint[] Empty = Array.Empty<MetricDataPoint>();\n\n        public bool CanHandle(in WorkActivityEvent workEvent)\n            => workEvent.Activity == ActivityType.Focus;\n\n        public ValueTask<IReadOnlyCollection<MetricDataPoint>> ComputeAsync(\n            WorkActivityEvent     private static readonly MetricDataPoint[] Empty = Array.Empty<MetricDataPoint>();\n\n        private readonly double _defaultHourlyRateUsd;\n\n        public MeetingCostIndexStrategy(double       public bool TryResolve(\n            WorkActivityEvent            workEvent,\n            [NotNullWhen(true)]\n            out IMetricComputationStrategy? strategy)\n        {\n        }\n            }\n\n            strategy = null;\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// In-memory metric sink (primarily for unit tests and local        IReadOnlyCollection<MetricDataPoint> metrics,\n            CancellationToken                     cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            _buffer.AddRange(metrics);\n -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    public sealed class EventTransformationPipeline : IAsyncDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly                 _sink;\n        private readonly ILogger                                       _log = Log.ForContext<EventTransformationPipeline>();\n        private readonly CancellationTokenSource                        _cts = new();\n\n        public EventTransformationPipeline(\n            StrategyRegistry strategyRegistry,\n            IMetricSink      metricSink,\n            int {\n                    BoundedCapacity = boundedCapacity\n                });\n\n            _compute = new TransformManyBlock<WorkActivityEvent, MetricDataPoint>(\n                async (workEvent)             return await strategy.ComputeAsync(\n                                workEvent,\n                                _cts.Token);\n                        }\n\n         {\n                        await metricSink.WriteAsync(\n                            new[] { metric },\n                            _cts.Token);\n                    }\n          metric);\n                        // In production, failed metrics would be forwarded to a DLQ.\n                    }\n                },\n                new ExecutionDataflowBlockOptions\n PropagateCompletion = true });\n            _compute.LinkTo(_sink,                     new DataflowLinkOptions { PropagateCompletion = true });\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Enqueues a new event for processing. {\n            if (!_input.Post(workEvent))\n            {\n                await _input.SendAsync(workEvent, cancellationToken);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Signals graceful shutdown and waits until all buffered work completes.\n        /// </summary>\n        public async Task CompleteAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n (cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)\n            {\n                _cts.Cancel();\n                throw;\n            }\n        }\n\n        public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n        {\n            _cts.Cancel();\n            _input.Complete();\n\n            try\n            {\n                await _sink.Completion;\n            }\n            catch\n            {\n                // Swallow any in-flight exceptions on dispose.\n            }\n\n            _cts.Dispose();\n        }\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n The orchestrator resolves a concrete IKpiComputationStrategy\n//  for the requested KPI, fans the work out across multiple threads (or\n//  reserved-concurrency Lambda instances, depending on runtime settings),\n//  handles transient failures with an exponential back-off policy, and finally\n//  streams the normalized result back to the Data-Lake.\n//\n//  The class is intentionally generic and can be reused from both the\n//  stream-processing (real-time) and batch-processing (historical back-fill)\n//  pipelines.\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Concurrent;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing System.Net;\nusing System.Text.Json;\nusing System.Threading;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\nusing Amazon;\nusing Amazon.Runtime;\nusing Amazon.S3;\nusing Amazon.S3.Model;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;\nusing PulseOps.Warehouse.Common;\nusing PulseOps.Warehouse.Contracts;\nusing PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations.Strategies;\n\nnamespace PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Orchestrates KPI computations by delegating the heavy lifting to a pool of\n    /// <see cref=\"IKpiComputationStrategy\"/> implementations.  Responsibilities:\n    ///\n    /// • Resolve the correct strategy for a given <see cref=\"KpiRequest\"/>\n    /// • Run the computation across a configurable degree of parallelism\n    /// • Persist results to the Lake (S3) using a structured partition scheme\n    /// • Emit CloudWatch observable metrics & traces\n    /// • Provide robust error-handling / back-off semantics\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class KpiComputationOrchestrator : IKpiComputationOrchestrator\n    {\n        private const string DefaultRegion = \"us-east-1\";\n        private readonly IKpiComputationStrategyRegistry _strategyRegistry;\n        private readonly IAmazonS3 _s3Client;\n        private readonly ILogger<KpiComputationOrchestrator> _logger;\n        private readonly OrchestratorOptions _options;\n\n        public KpiComputationOrchestrator(\n            IKpiComputationStrategyRegistry strategyRegistry,\n            ILogger<KpiComputationOrchestrator> logger,\n            OrchestratorOptions? options = null,\n            IAmazonS3? s3Client = null)\n        {\n            _strategyRegistry = strategyRegistry ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(strategyRegistry));\n            _logger = logger ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(logger));\n            _options = options ?? OrchestratorOptions.FromEnvironment();\n            _s3Client = s3Client ?? new AmazonS3Client(FallbackCredentialsFactory.GetCredentials(), RegionEndpoint.GetBySystemName(DefaultRegion));\n        }\n\n        public async Task<KpiBatchResult> ProcessBatchAsync(\n            IEnumerable<KpiRequest> requests,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (requests == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(requests));\n\n            var requestList = requests.ToList();\n            _logger.LogInformation(\"Processing {Count} KPI requests\", requestList.Count);\n\n            var throttler = new SemaphoreSlim(_options.DegreeOfParallelism);\n            var resultCollector = new ConcurrentBag<KpiComputationResult>();\n            var failures = new ConcurrentBag<KpiComputationFailure>();\n\n            var tasks = requestList.Select(async request =>\n            {\n                await throttler.WaitAsync(cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                try\n                {\n                    await ExecuteWithRetryAsync(request, cancellationToken, async () =>\n                    {\n                        var strategy = _strategyRegistry.Resolve(request.KpiName);\n                        _logger.LogDebug(\"Resolved strategy {Strategy} for KPI {Kpi}\",\n                            strategy.GetType().Name, request.KpiName);\n\n                        var result = await strategy.ComputeAsync(request, cancellationToken)\n                            .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                        await PersistToLakeAsync(result, cancellationToken)\n                            .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                        _logger.LogInformation(\"Successfully processed KPI {Kpi} for Tenant {TenantId}\",\n                            request.KpiName, request.TenantId);\n\n                        resultCollector.Add(result);\n                    }).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger.LogError(ex, \"Failed to process KPI {Kpi} for Tenant {TenantId}\",\n                        request.KpiName, request.TenantId);\n\n                    failures.Add(new KpiComputationFailure(request, ex));\n                }\n                finally\n                {\n                    throttler.Release();\n                }\n            }).ToArray();\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(tasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            return new KpiBatchResult(resultCollector.ToArray(), failures.ToArray());\n        }\n\n        #region Helpers\n\n        private async Task ExecuteWithRetryAsync(\n            KpiRequest request,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken,\n            Func<Task> action)\n        {\n            var attempt = 0;\n            var delay = _options.InitialBackoff;\n\n            while (true)\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n                    await action().ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    return; // success\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex) when (IsTransient(ex) && attempt < _options.MaxRetryAttempts)\n                {\n                    attempt++;\n                    _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                        \"[Attempt {Attempt}/{MaxAttempts}] Transient failure while processing {Kpi} (Tenant {TenantId}). Retrying in {DelayMs}ms…\",\n                        attempt, _options.MaxRetryAttempts, request.KpiName, request.TenantId, delay.TotalMilliseconds);\n\n                    await Task.Delay(delay, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    delay = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(delay.TotalMilliseconds * _options.BackoffMultiplier);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        private static bool IsTransient(Exception ex)\n        {\n            return ex switch\n            {\n                AmazonServiceException ase\n                    when ase.StatusCode is HttpStatusCode.InternalServerError or HttpStatusCode.ServiceUnavailable => true,\n                TimeoutException => true,\n                _ => false\n            };\n        }\n\n        private async Task PersistToLakeAsync(\n            KpiComputationResult result,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n        {\n            // Example partition: kpi_name=FocusTimeRatio/year=2023/month=08/day=14/hour=13/<tenantId>.json\n            var (bucket, key) = GetS3LocationForResult(result);\n\n            using var stream = JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(result).AsStream();\n            var putRequest = new PutObjectRequest\n            {\n                BucketName = bucket,\n                Key = key,\n                InputStream = stream,\n                ContentType = \"application/json\",\n                AutoCloseStream = true\n            };\n\n            await _s3Client.PutObjectAsync(putRequest, cancellationToken)\n                .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            _logger.LogDebug(\"Persisted KPI {Kpi} result to s3://{Bucket}/{Key}\", result.KpiName, bucket, key);\n        }\n\n        private (string Bucket, string Key) GetS3LocationForResult(KpiComputationResult result)\n        {\n            var timestamp = result.GeneratedAtUtc;\n            var key =\n                $\"{result.KpiName}/year={timestamp:yyyy}/month={timestamp:MM}/day={timestamp:dd}/hour={timestamp:HH}/{result.TenantId}.json\";\n\n            return (_options.S3Bucket, key);\n        }\n\n        #endregion\n    }\n\n    #region Options & Contracts\n\n    public sealed record OrchestratorOptions(\n        int DegreeOfParallelism,\n        int MaxRetryAttempts,\n        TimeSpan                   }\n                    }\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger?.LogError(ex, \"Failed while enqueuing KPIs for flush.\");\n                }\n                finally\n                {\n                    channel.Writer.Complete();\n                }\n            }, ct);\n\n            // Consumer: write out via provided writer (if streaming), else just log.\n            await foreach (var kpi in channel.Reader.ReadAllAsync(ct)\n                                                    .ConfigureAwait(false))\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    if (writer is not null)\n                    {\n                        await writer.WriteAsync(kpi, ct)\n                                    .ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    }\n                    else\n                    {\n                        _logger?.LogInformation(\n                            \"KPI flushed (dry-run): {kpi}\",\n                            JsonSerializer.Serialize(kpi));\n                    }\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger?.LogWarning(\n                        ex,\n                        \"Failed to persist KPI for EmployeeId={EmployeeId} Date={Date}\",\n                        kpi.EmployeeId,\n                        kpi.Date.ToString(\"yyyy-MM-dd\",\n                                          CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        #endregion\n\n        #region Aggregate – private helper\n\n        private sealed class Aggregate\n        {\n            public string   EmployeeId     { get; }\n            public DateTime Date           { get; }\n            public double   FocusMinutes   { get; private set; }\n            public double   MeetingMinutes { get; private set; }\n\n            private Aggregate(string employeeId, DateTime date)\n            {\n                EmployeeId = employeeId;\n                Date       = date;\n            }\n\n            public static Aggregate New(string employeeId, DateTime date, RawProductivityEvent evt)\n            {\n                var agg = new Aggregate(employeeId, date);\n                return agg.Add(evt);\n            }\n\n            /// <summary>\n            /// Thread-safe accumulation of a new event into the aggregate.\n            /// </summary>\n            public Aggregate Add(RawProductivityEvent evt)\n            {\n                var minutes = evt.Duration.TotalMinutes;\n                switch (evt.Kind)\n                {\n                    case EventKind.FocusSession:\n                        InterlockedAdd(ref FocusMinutes, minutes);\n                        break;\n\n                    case EventKind.Meeting:\n                        InterlockedAdd(ref MeetingMinutes, minutes);\n                        break;\n                }\n\n                return this;\n            }\n\n            public FocusTimeRatioKpi ToKpi()\n            {\n                var total = FocusMinutes + MeetingMinutes;\n                var ratio = total == 0 ? 0 : FocusMinutes / total;\n\n                return new FocusTimeRatioKpi(\n                    EmployeeId,\n                    Date,\n                    Math.Round(FocusMinutes,   2),\n                    Math.Round(MeetingMinutes, 2),\n                    Math.Round(ratio,          4));\n            }\n\n            private static void InterlockedAdd(ref double location, double value)\n            {\n                double newCurrentValue, currentValue;\n                do\n                {\n                    currentValue   = location;\n                    newCurrentValue = currentValue + value;\n                }\n                while (Math.Abs(\n                           Interlocked.CompareExchange(\n                               ref location,\n                               newCurrentValue,\n                               currentValue) - currentValue) > double.Epsilon);\n            }\n        }\n\n        #endregion\n\n        public void Dispose()\n        {\n            _flushTimer.Dispose();\n            // One last synchronous flush in case app is shutting down abruptly.\n            FlushBufferAsync().GetAwaiter().GetResult();\n        }\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":303,"startLine":1,"totalLines":303}}}

strategy.TransformAsync(rawEvent, ctx.CancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                // Persist KPI record (omitted — could be S3, Redshift, Firehose, etc.).\n                logger.LogDebug($\"Successfully transformed event → KPI '{kpiRecord.KpiName}'.\");\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                logger.LogError($\"[StrategyFail] Strategy={strategy.GetType().Name} — {ex}\");\n                await _dlqPublisher.PublishAsync(DlqMessage.FromDeadLetter(record, ex), ctx.CancellationToken)\n                                   .ConfigureAwait(false);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private static RawEvent DeserializeRecord(KinesisEvent.KinesisEventRecord record)\n        {\n            var json = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(record.Kinesis.Data.Span);\n            var document = JsonDocument.Parse(json);\n            var root = document.RootElement;\n\n            return new RawEvent(\n                ContentType: root.GetProperty(\"contentType\").GetString() ?? \"application/unknown\",\n                Payload: root.GetProperty(\"payload\"),\n                Timestamp: root.GetProperty(\"timestamp\").GetDateTime());\n        }\n\n        #endregion\n    }\n\n    #region === Strategy Pattern Infrastructure =======================================================================\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Lightweight service-locator for <see cref=\"ITransformationStrategy\"/> implementations.\n    /// Scans the current assembly only once and caches results in a thread-safe dictionary.\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class StrategyResolver : IStrategyResolver\n    {\n        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, ITransformationStrategy> _cache = new();\n\n        public StrategyResolver()\n        {\n            // Pre-warm the cache so that we fail fast on startup if multiple\n            // strategies claim to handle the same content type.\n            var implementations = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly()\n                                          .GetTypes()\n                                          .Where(t => !t.IsAbstract && typeof(ITransformationStrategy).IsAssignableFrom(t));\n\n            foreach (var type in implementations)\n            {\n                var instance = (ITransformationStrategy)Activator.CreateInstance(type)!;\n                foreach (var contentType in instance.SupportedContentTypes)\n                {\n                    if (!_cache.TryAdd(contentType, instance))\n                    {\n                        throw new InvalidOperationException(\n                            $\"Multiple transformation strategies registered for content type '{contentType}'.\");\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        public ITransformationStrategy Resolve(string contentType)\n        {\n            if (!_cache.TryGetValue(contentType, out var strategy))\n            {\n                throw new UnknownContentTypeException(contentType);\n            }\n\n            return strategy;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Thrown when no strategy is found for the specified content type.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class UnknownContentTypeException : Exception\n    {\n        public UnknownContentTypeException(string contentType)\n            : base($\"No transformation strategy registered for content type '{contentType}'.\")\n        { }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Abstraction for locating the correct transformation strategy.\n    /// </summary>\n    public interface IStrategyResolver\n    {\n        ITransformationStrategy Resolve(string contentType);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Common interface for KPI transformation strategies.  Each implementation\n    /// must declare which content types it can consume.\n    /// </summary>\n    public interface ITransformationStrategy\n    {\n        IReadOnlyCollection<string> SupportedContentTypes { get; }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Transforms a raw telemetry event into a normalized KPI record.\n        /// </summary>\n        Task<KpiRecord> TransformAsync(RawEvent rawEvent, CancellationToken token);\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region === Concrete Strategy Examples ============================================================================\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Example strategy that derives “Focus Time” KPI from calendar metadata.\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class CalendarFocusTimeStrategy : ITransformationStrategy\n    {\n        public IReadOnlyCollection<string> SupportedContentTypes { get; } =\n            new[] { \"application/vnd.pulseops.calendar+json\" };\n\n        public Task<KpiRecord> TransformAsync(RawEvent rawEvent, CancellationToken token)\n        {\n            // Domain-specific parsing stripped down for brevity.\n            var meetingDurationMinutes = rawEvent.Payload.GetProperty(\"meetingDurationMinutes\").GetInt32();\n            var userId = rawEvent.Payload.GetProperty(\"userId\").GetString()!;\n\n            var focusTime = 480 - meetingDurationMinutes; // 8h work day.\n\n            var kpiPayload = JsonSerializer.SerializeToElement(new\n            {\n                userId,\n                focusTimeMinutes = focusTime,\n                computationDate = rawEvent.Timestamp.Date\n            });\n\n            return Task.FromResult(new KpiRecord(\"focus_time\", kpiPayload));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Example strategy computing “Deployment Lead Time” KPI from CI/CD events.\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy : ITransformationStrategy\n    {\n        public IReadOnlyCollection<string> SupportedContentTypes { get; } =\n            new[] { \"application/vnd.pulseops.cicd+json\" };\n\n        public Task<KpiRecord> TransformAsync(RawEvent rawEvent, CancellationToken token)\n        {\n            var queuedAt = rawEvent.Payload.GetProperty(\"queuedAt\").GetDateTime();\n            var deployedAt = rawEvent.Payload.GetProperty(\"deployedAt\").GetDateTime();\n\n            var leadTimeMinutes = (deployedAt - queuedAt).TotalMinutes;\n\n            var pipelineId = rawEvent.Payload.GetProperty(\"pipelineId\").GetString()!;\n\n            var kpiPayload = JsonSerializer.SerializeToElement(new\n            {\n                pipelineId,\n                leadTimeMinutes,\n                dequeuedDate = deployedAt.Date\n            });\n\n            return Task.FromResult(new KpiRecord(\"deployment_lead_time\", kpiPayload));\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region === Dead Letter Queue Components ==========================================================================\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Simple abstraction for publishing dead-letter messages.\n    /// </summary>\n    public interface IDlqPublisher\n    {\n        Task PublishAsync(DlqMessage message, CancellationToken token);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// SNS-backed DLQ publisher used in production.  Topic ARN is provided via environment variable.\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class SnsDlqPublisher : IDlqPublisher\n    {\n        private static readonly string TopicArn =\n            Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(\"DLQ_TOPIC_ARN\")\n            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(\"DLQ_TOPIC_ARN environment variable not set.\");\n\n        private readonly mocked IAmazonSimpleNotificationService.\n        public SnsDlqPublisher(IAmazonSimpleNotificationService snsClient)\n        {\n            _snsClient = snsClient;\n        }\n\n        public async Task PublishAsync(DlqMessage message, CancellationToken token)\n        {\n            var request = new PublishRequest\n            {\n                TopicArn = TopicArn,\n                Message = JsonSerializer.Serialize(message)\n            };\n\n            await _snsClient.PublishAsync(request, token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Canonical schema for dead-letter messages.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed record DlqMessage(\n        string Source,\n        string Reason,\n        object OriginalEvent)\n    {\n        public static DlqMessage FromDeadLetter(KinesisEvent.KinesisEventRecord record, Exception ex) =>\n            new(\n                Source: \"StreamTransformationCoordinator\",\n                Reason: ex.ToString(),\n                OriginalEvent: new\n                {\n                    record.EventId,\n                    PartitionKey = record.Kinesis.PartitionKey,\n      /// <summary>\n        /// Entry-point: Transform a batch of raw records.\n        /// Safe-for-parallel execution.\n        /// </summary>\n        /// <returns>List of successfully transformed     {\n            var tasks = rawBatch.Select(raw => TransformSingleAsync(raw, token));\n            var transformed = await Task.WhenAll(tasks);\n\n            // Filter out nulls (failed records 2),\n                Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n                {\n                    [\"currency\"] = \"USD\"\n                },\n                RawPayload = JsonDocument.Parse(envelope.Payload.GetRawText())\n            };\n\n            return Task.FromResult(record);\n        }\n    }\n\n    internal sealed class DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy : IMetricTransformationStrategy\n    {\n        public string MetricType => \"deployment_lead_time\";\n\n        public Task<MetricRecord> TransformAsync(EventEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken token)\n        {\n            var commitTs  = envelope.Payload.GetProperty(\"commitTimestamp\").GetDateTime();\n            var deployTs  = envelope.Payload.GetProperty(\"deployTimestamp\").GetDateTime();\n\n            var leadTimeHrs = (deployTs - commitTs).TotalHours;\n\n            var record = new MetricRecord\n            {\n                MetricType = MetricType,\n                TenantId   = envelope.TenantId,\n                PeriodEnd  = deployTs,\n                Value      = Math.Round(leadTimeHrs, 2),\n                Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n                {\n                    [\"repository\"] = envelope.Payload.GetProperty(\"repository\").GetString() ?? \"unknown\"\n                },\n                RawPayload = JsonDocument.Parse(envelope.Payload.GetRawText())\n            };\n\n            return Task.FromResult(record);\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Output Channel\n\n    public interface IMetricPublisher\n    {\n        Task PublishAsync(MetricRecord record, CancellationToken token);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Persists metrics to S3 as newline-delimited JSON, partitioned by tenant & date.\n    /// Glue crawlers project the schema automatically.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class S3MetricPublisher : IMetricPublisher\n    {\n        private const string BucketName = \"pulseops-warehouse-metrics\";\n        private readonly IAmazonS3 _s3;\n        private readonly ILogger<S3MetricPublisher> _logger;\n\n        public S3MetricPublisher(IAmazonS3 s3, ILogger<S3MetricPublisher> logger)\n        {\n            _s3    = s3;\n            _logger = logger;\n        }\n\n        public async Task PublishAsync(MetricRecord record, CancellationToken token)\n        {\n            var key = BuildObjectKey(record);\n            var body = JsonSerializer.Serialize(record) + \"\\n\"; // NDJSON\n\n            try\n            {\n                var response = await _s3.PutObjectAsync(new PutObjectRequest\n                {\n                    BucketName  = BucketName,\n                    Key         = key,\n                    ContentBody = body,\n                    ContentType = \"application/x-ndjson\",\n                    ServerSideEncryptionMethod = ServerSideEncryptionMethod.AES256\n                }, token);\n\n                if (response.HttpStatusCode != HttpStatusCode.OK)\n                {\n                    _logger.LogError(\"Failed to upload metric. S3 returned {StatusCode} for Key={Key}\", response.HttpStatusCode, key);\n                    throw new Exception($\"S3 upload failed with status {response.HttpStatusCode}\");\n                }\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                _logger.LogError(ex, \"Error publishing metric to S3.  Tenant={Tenant} MetricType={MetricType}\", record.TenantId, record.MetricType);\n                throw;\n            }\n        }\n\n        private static string BuildObjectKey(MetricRecord record)\n        {\n            var date = record.PeriodEnd;\n            return $\"{record.TenantId}/metric_type={record.MetricType}/year={date:yyyy}/month={date:MM}/day={date:dd}/{Guid.NewGuid()}.json\";\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n}\n```","numLines":357,"startLine":1,"totalLines":357}}}

Resolution ────────────────────────────────\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Attribute used by reflection-based discovery of transformation strategies.\n    /// </summary>\n    [AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Class, Inherited = false)]\n    public sealed class StrategyAttribute : Attribute\n    {\n        public StrategyAttribute(string key) => Key = key ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(key));\n\n        public string Key { get; }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Thread-safe registry that caches concrete strategy instances keyed by event type.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class TransformationStrategyRegistry\n    {\n        private readonly ILogger<TransformationStrategyRegistry> _logger;\n        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, ITransformationStrategy> _cache = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);\n\n        public TransformationStrategyRegistry(ILogger<TransformationStrategyRegistry> logger)\n        {\n            _logger = logger ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(logger));\n            LoadFromAssemblies(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies());\n        }\n\n        ///   if (_cache.TryGetValue(eventType, out var strategy)) return strategy;\n\n            throw new KeyNotFoundException($\"No transformation strategy registered for event type '{eventType}'.\");\n      _logger.LogWarning(\"Strategy {Key} already registered – overriding instance.\", strategy.StrategyKey);\n                _cache[strategy.StrategyKey] = strategy;\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void LoadFromAssemblies(IEnumerable<Assembly> assemblies)\n        {\n            foreach (Assembly asm in assemblies.Where(a => !a.IsDynamic))\n            {\n                foreach (var (ITransformationStrategy)Activator.CreateInstance(type)!;\n                        Register(instance);\n                    }\n                    catch (Exception ex)\n                    {\n /// High-level orchestrator that fans-out raw events to the relevant strategy,\n    /// executes them in parallel with retry semantics, and fans-in KPI records.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class TransformationEngine\n    {\n        private static readonly AsyncRetryPolicy RetryPolicy = Policy\n            .Handle<Exception>(ex => ex is not OperationCanceledException)\n            .WaitAndRetryAsync(\n                retryCount: 3,\n                sleepDurationProvider: attempt => TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100 * Math.Pow(2, attempt)), // 100ms, 200ms, 400ms\n Retrying after {Delay}ms…\",\n                            attempt, ts.TotalMilliseconds);\n                    }\n                });\n\n        private readonly TransformationStrategyRegistry _registry;\n        private readonly ILogger<TransformationEngine> _logger;\n\n        public TransformationEngine(\n            TransformationStrategyRegistry registry,\n            ILogger<TransformationEngine> logger)\n        {\n            _registry = registry ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(registry));\n            _logger = logger ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(logger));\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Orchestrates transformation of a batch of raw events.\n        /// </summary>\n        public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<KpiRecord>> ExecuteBatchAsync(\n            IReadOnlyCollection<RawEvent> events,\n            CancellationToken token = default)\n        {\n            if (events is null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(events));\n\n            var kpiBag = new ConcurrentBag<KpiRecord>();\n            var stopwatch = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n\n            // Run transformations concurrently but keep within sane parallelism to protect lambda memory\n            await Parallel.ForEachAsync(events, new ParallelOptions\n            {\n                CancellationToken = token,\n                MaxDegreeOfParallelism = Environment.ProcessorCount // auto-scale based on memory\n            }, async (ev, ct) =>\n            {\n                // Strategy resolution (fast dictionary lookup)\n                ITransformationStrategy strategy;\n                try\n                {\n                    strategy = _registry.GetOrThrow(ev.EventType);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger.LogError(ex, \"Unable to find strategy for event type '{EventType}'. Dropping event.\", ev.EventType);\n                    return; // swallow – DLQ routing handled upstream\n                }\n\n                // Execute with retry & jitter\n                var context, ct);\n\n                foreach (var kpi in results)\n                {\n                    kpiBag.Add(kpi);\n                }\n            });\n\n            stopwatch.Stop();\n            _logger.LogInformation(\n                \"Processed {EventCount} event(s) into {KpiCount} KPI record(s) in {ElapsedMs} ms.\",\n                events.Count, kpiBag.Count, stopwatch.ElapsedMilliseconds);\n\n            return kpiBag.ToArray();\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Sample Strategies ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Calculates focus-time ratio from calendar events.\n    /// EventType = \"calendar_event\"\n    ///\n    /// Expects payload form:\n    /// {\n    ///     \"start\": {\n            // Basic validation\n            if (!raw.Payload.TryGetValue(\"start\", out var startObj) ||\n                !raw.Payload.TryGetValue(\"end\", out var endObj))\n            {\n                throw new InvalidOperationException(\"Invalid calendar event payload – missing start/end.\");\n            }\n\n            var start = DateTimeOffset.Parse(startObj.ToString()!);\n            var end   = DateTimeOffset.Parse(endObj.ToString()!);\n            var durationMinutes = (end - start).TotalMinutes;\n\n            // Example heuristic: meetings with <= 2 attendees are considered 'focus'\n            var attendees = raw.Payload.TryGetValue(\"attendees\", out var attendeeObj)\n                ?         {\n                    [\"source\"] = raw.SourceSystem,\n                    [\"isRecurring\"] = raw.Payload.TryGetValue(\"isRecurring\", out var r) && (bool)r\n                        ? \"true\"\n                        : \"false\"\n                });\n\n            return Task.FromResult<IReadOnlyCollection<KpiRecord>>(new[] { kpi });\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///        public Task<IReadOnlyCollection<KpiRecord>> TransformAsync(RawEvent raw, CancellationToken token)\n        {\n            if (!raw.Payload.TryGetValue(\"commit_timestamp\", out var commitTsObj) They should\n        ///     never mutate the incoming context.\n        /// </remarks>\n        ValueTask<KpiResult> ComputeAsync(\n            KpiComputationContext context,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);\n    }\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Attribute-based discovery\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Decorating a class with <see cref=\"KpiStrategyAttribute\"/> auto-registers it with\n    ///     <see cref=\"KpiStrategyRegistry\"/> at startup time.\n    /// </summary>\n    [AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Class, Inherited = false)]\n    public sealed class KpiStrategyAttribute : Attribute\n    {\n        public KpiStrategyAttribute(string kpiName) => KpiName = kpiName;\n\n        public string KpiName { get; Used by feature-flag rollouts.\n        /// </summary>\n        public void Upsert(IKpiComputationStrategy strategy)\n        {\n            _strategies.AddOrUpdate(strategy.KpiName, strategy, (_, __) => strategy);\n            _logger.LogInformation(\"Registered KPI strategy: {KpiName} ({Type})\",\n                strategy.KpiName, strategy.GetType().FullName);\n        }\n\n        private void DiscoverStrategies()\n        {\n            var stopwatch = System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.StartNew();\n\n            // Scan all loaded assemblies (excluding Microsoft / System to speed things up).\n            var assemblies = AppDomain.CurrentDomain\n                                      .GetAssemblies()\n                                      .Where(a => !a.FullName.StartsWith(\"System.\", StringComparison.Ordinal) &&\n                                                  !a.FullName.StartsWith(\"Microsoft.\", StringComparison.Ordinal))\n                                      .ToArray();\n\n            int count = 0;\n            foreach (var assembly in assemblies)\n            {\n                foreach (var candidate in assembly.GetTypes())\n                {\n                    if (candidate.IsAbstract ||\n                        candidate.IsInterface ||\n                        !typeof(IKpiComputationStrategy).IsAssignableFrom(candidate))\n                        continue;\n\n                    var attribute = candidate.GetCustomAttribute<KpiStrategyAttribute>();\n                    if (attribute is null)\n                        continue;\n\n                    // Instantiate via Activator; strategy must have a public parameterless ctor.\n                    if (Activator.CreateInstance(candidate) is not IKpiComputationStrategy instance)\n                    {\n                        _logger.LogWarning(\"Failed to instantiate KPI strategy '{StrategyType}'. Skipping.\",\n                            candidate.FullName);\n                        continue;\n                    }\n\n                    Upsert(instance);\n                    count++;\n                }\n            }\n\n            stopwatch.Stop();\n            _logger.LogInformation(\"KPI strategy discovery complete — {Count} strategies loaded in {ElapsedMs}ms.\",\n                count, stopwatch.ElapsedMilliseconds);\n        }\n    }\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Orchestrator\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Orchestrates KPI computations in parallel, respecting cancellation tokens and\n    ///     aggregating failures in a structured way.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class KpiComputationOrchestrator\n    {\n        private readonly KpiStrategyRegistry _registry;\n        private readonly ILogger<KpiComputationOrchestrator> _logger;\n\n        public KpiComputationOrchestrator(\n            KpiStrategyRegistry registry,\n            ILogger<KpiComputationOrchestrator> logger)\n        {\n            _registry = registry;\n            _logger = logger;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        ///     Computes a batch of KPI names against the same data context, executing work in\n        ///     parallel while enforcing a configurable degree of parallelism.\n        /// </summary>\n        /// <param name=\"kpiNames\">The KPI identifiers to compute.</param>\n        /// <param name=\"context\">Shared context passed to each strategy.</param>\n        /// <param name=\"maxParallelism\">Limits in-flight computations (1-32 recommended).</param>\n        /// <param name=\"cancellationToken\">Token to cancel the entire batch.</param>\n        /// <returns>Collection of <see cref=\"KpiResult\"/> plus any failures (exceptions).</returns>\n        public async Task<(IReadOnlyList<KpiResult> Results, IReadOnlyList<Exception> Errors)> ComputeBatchAsync(\n            IEnumerable<string> kpiNames,\n            KpiComputationContext context,\n            int maxParallelism = 8,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (context is null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(context));\n            if (kpiNames is null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(kpiNames));\n            if (maxParallelism is < 1 or > 32)\n                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(maxParallelism),\n                    \"maxParallelism must be between 1 and 32.\");\n\n            var resultBag = new ConcurrentBag<KpiResult>();\n            var errorBag = new ConcurrentBag<Exception>();\n\n            using var semaphore = new SemaphoreSlim(maxParallelism);\n\n            var tasks = kpiNames.Select(async kpiName =>\n            {\n                await semaphore.WaitAsync(cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                try\n                {\n                    var strategy = _registry.Get(kpiName);\n                    var result = await strategy.ComputeAsync(context, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    resultBag.Add(result);\n                }\n                catch (OperationCanceledException)\n                {\n                    // Bubble up cancellation — don’t treat as error.\n                    throw;\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    errorBag.Add(ex);\n                    _logger.LogError(ex, \"Failed to compute KPI '{Kpi}'.\", kpiName);\n                }\n                finally\n                {\n                    semaphore.Release();\n                }\n            });\n\n            try\n            {\n                await Task.WhenAll(tasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            }\n            catch (OperationCanceledException)\n            {\n                // caller cancelled — swallow here, propagate via token.\n            }\n\n            return (resultBag.ToArray(), errorBag.ToArray());\n        }\n    }\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Sample Strategies\n    // NOTE: These sample implementations are intentionally simple.  Production strategies would,\n    //       for example, query Athena / S3 parquet, leverage Apache Arrow, or perform complex ML\n    //       inference via SageMaker endpoints.  The samples demonstrate the pluggability only.\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Focus-time ratio = (Coding/Deep work minutes) ÷ Total minutes in analytic window.\n    ///     Requires that the context includes:\n    ///         - \"DeepWorkMinutes\" (double)\n    ///         - \"WindowMinutes\"   (double)\n    /// </summary>\n    [KpiStrategy(\"focus_time_ratio\")]\n    public sealed class FocusTimeRatioStrategy : IKpiComputationStrategy\n    {\n        public string KpiName => \"focus_time_ratio\";\n\n        public ValueTask<KpiResult> ComputeAsync(\n            KpiComputationContext context,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (!context.Data.TryGetValue(\"DeepWorkMinutes\", out var deepWork) ||\n                !context.Data.TryGetValue(\"WindowMinutes\", out var window))\n                throw new ArgumentException(\"Required keys missing: DeepWorkMinutes or WindowMinutes\");\n\n            double deepWorkMinutes = Convert.ToDouble(deepWork);\n            double windowMinutes = Convert.ToDouble(window);\n\n            if (windowMinutes <= 0)\n                throw new DivideByZeroException(\"WindowMinutes must be > 0\");\n\n            var ratio = deepWorkMinutes / windowMinutes;\n\n            var metadata = new Dictionary<string, string>\n            {\n                [\"unit\"] = \"ratio\",\n                [\"deepWorkMinutes\"] = deepWorkMinutes.ToString(\"F0\"),\n                [\"windowMinutes\"] = windowMinutes.ToString(\"F0\")\n            };\n\n            var result = new KpiResult(\n                KpiName,\n                Math.Round(ratio, 4),\n                DateTime.UtcNow,\n                metadata);\n\n            return ValueTask.FromResult(result);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Meeting cost index = Σ(MeetingMinutes × AttendeeHourlyRate) ÷ WindowMinutes.\n    ///     Requires the context includes a sequences of meetings under \"Meetings\":\n    ///         IReadOnlyList&lt;(double Minutes, double HourlyRate)&gt;\n    /// </summary>\n    [KpiStrategy(\"meeting_cost_index\")]\n    public sealed class MeetingCostIndexStrategy : IKpiComputationStrategy\n    {\n        public string KpiName => \"meeting_cost_index\";\n\n        public ValueTask<KpiResult> ComputeAsync(\n            KpiComputationContext context,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (!context.Data.TryGetValue(\"Meetings\", out var rawMeetings) ||\n                rawMeetings is not IEnumerable<(double Minutes, double HourlyRate)> meetings)\n                throw new ArgumentException(\"Meeting data missing or invalid.\");\n\n            var totalCost = meetings.Sum(m => m.Minutes * (m.HourlyRate / 60.0));\n            var windowMinutes = (context.WindowEnd - context.WindowStart).TotalMinutes;\n\n            if (windowMinutes <= 0)\n                throw new DivideByZeroException(\"Invalid analytic window.\");\n\n            double index = totalCost / windowMinutes;\n\n            var metadata = new Dictionary<string, string>\n            {\n ValueTask.FromResult(result);\n        }\n    }\n    #endregion\n}\n```","numLines":348,"startLine":1,"totalLines":348}}}

4;\n    public bool EnableBackfillLambda  { get; init; } = true;\n    public int  BatchWindowSeconds    { get; init; } = 900;  // 15-minute minimum window\n\n    public void Validate()\n    {\n
